# Gen5.1 POC Progress Log

## Purpose

This log is the concise memory of the Gen5/Gen5.1 proof-of-concept pathway. It records the conceptual POCs we have run, the question each POC answered, the primary artifact folders, and the practical readout or next decision.

This is not a performance leaderboard and not an allocation approval record. Unless an entry explicitly says otherwise, every result here is research/inspection evidence only.

## Status Legend

- `INSPECTION`: useful artifact or audit surface; not accepted research or allocation evidence.
- `PROMISING`: worth retesting, expanding, or keeping as a contender.
- `DEPRECATED`: unlikely to remain a primary path, based on current inspection.
- `BLOCKED`: needs data, code, or operator decision before continuing.
- `ACCEPTED_DIRECTION`: operator-approved direction for further research work; still not live advice or deployment evidence.

## Closeout Rule

At the conclusion of each future POC or research-inspection slice, add or update one entry here with:

- the question;
- the scope;
- the main artifact folder or report;
- the short readout;
- the next decision or STOP state.

Selection Policy x Basket Archetype Screen | `PROMISING` | Does the direct-spec versus pooled-family selection-policy fork behave differently across live-like, long-history high-beta, and ETF/sector baskets when context/PCA/state/grid are held fixed? | [basket screen packet](../runs/research_workbench/selpol_basket/selpol_basket_20260703), [top-level report](../runs/research_workbench/selpol_basket/selpol_basket_20260703/selection_policy_basket_archetype_report.md), [wrapper](../scripts/inspect/run_selection_policy_basket_archetype_screen.R), [summary deck](../presentations/gen5_selection_policy_basket_archetype_screen.pptx) | Ran active-plus-risk context, behavioral-pool PCA, 3x3 quantile states, and broad Gen5.1 research families with Gen4 daily parameter breadth across three basket archetypes. | 32. Selection Policy x Context Philosophy Screen | `PROMISING` | When basket type is held to long-history high-beta or ETF/sector proxies, does regime-context construction change the direct-spec versus pooled-family answer, and did any lane beat an equal-weight hold of the same basket? | [context-philosophy packet](../runs/research_workbench/selpol_context/selpol_context_20260703), [top-level report](../runs/research_workbench/selpol_context/selpol_context_20260703/selection_policy_context_philosophy_report.md), [benchmark visuals](../runs/research_workbench/selpol_context/selpol_context_20260703/benchmark_visuals), [performance audit](../runs/research_workbench/selpol_context/selpol_context_20260703/performance_audit/HB_broad_risk_no_vxx), [wrapper](../scripts/inspect/run_selection_policy_context_philosophy_screen.R), [summary deck](../presentations/gen5_selection_policy_context_philosophy_screen.pptx), [audit-copy deck](../presentations/gen5_selection_policy_context_philosophy_screen_audit_update.pptx) | Superseded the prior basket-archetype pilot as the cleaner evidence surface rather than deleting it. The new screen removed `VXX` from behavioral-pool context, dropped the SOFI/PLTR recent-history lane to preserve long historical windows, replaced discontinued/partial `SQ` context with `LRCX`, and compared four context recipes: broad risk, archetype matched, large diverse, and size-matched diverse. Raw trace returns still show meaningful policy/context interaction: high-beta favored pooled-family in matched, large-diverse, and size-matched-diverse contexts, while ETF/sector lanes were more direct-friendly outside broad risk. Selection-map agreement remained partial, not interchangeable. The local benchmark lens reframed the readout: every lane had negative mean excess return versus equal-weight basket hold. Best mean excess was still negative for high beta (`HB / matched / pooled`, `-13.7 pp`, `1/6` beat windows) and ETF/sector (`ETF / broad risk / pooled`, `-6.4 pp`, `2/6` beat windows). A focused performance audit of `HB_broad_risk_no_vxx` showed that 2020Q3 underperformance was not mainly catastrophic trade selection: direct had `13` wins / `3` losses and pooled had `15` wins / `2` losses, but mean exposure was only `35.5%` direct and `48.3%` pooled while the basket surged. The audit now adds selected-authority heatmaps showing that no-trade remains dominant in many state/asset cells, pooled-family compresses authority toward pullback/RSI/EMA families, and direct-spec keeps more idiosyncratic candidate families alive. Keep selection policy and context philosophy explicit, but make the next slice alpha-diagnostic: separate exposure, upside participation, downside avoidance, state accountability, and selection-policy effects. |
| 33. EMA Trend Participation Troubleshooting Probe | `PROMISING` | If underexposure during upside windows is a failure mode, do more aggressive/carrying `ema_trend` variants improve participation without obvious overtrading? | [EMA-only baseline packet](../runs/research_workbench/selpol_context/selpol_context_e0), [compact probe packet](../runs/research_workbench/selpol_context/selpol_context_e2), [comparison report](../runs/research_workbench/selpol_context/selpol_context_e2/ema_trend_participation_comparison/HB_broad_risk_no_vxx/ema_trend_participation_comparison_report.md), [comparison visuals](../runs/research_workbench/selpol_context/selpol_context_e2/ema_trend_participation_comparison/HB_broad_risk_no_vxx), [summary deck](../presentations/gen5_ema_trend_participation_probe.pptx) | Used `HB_broad_risk_no_vxx` as a troubleshooting testbed and deliberately narrowed candidate families to `ema_trend,no_trade` before spending full-family compute. A broad all-combinations EMA probe was too slow, so the implemented compact preset preserves Gen4-default EMA trend specs and adds targeted fast/carry variants (`above_slow` or 1-bar positive slope entries, `cross_below` exits). Versus the EMA-only default baseline, compact variants increased selected EMA states by `+4.4 pp` direct and `+7.2 pp` pooled, exposure by `+2.8 pp` direct and `+5.4 pp` pooled, and mean replay proxy by `+0.9 pp` direct and `+1.2 pp` pooled. Window-level deltas were mixed, including worse 2019Q3 and pooled 2020Q3, so this is a promising mechanism probe, not a default-grid promotion. Next decision: test a pruned compact EMA subset inside the full-family lane only if the operator wants to spend that compute. |
| 34. Gen4-Equivalence Forensic Screens | `PROMISING` | Was the remembered Gen4 benchmark-beating behavior due to a real design difference, or were we comparing Gen4 artifacts against unlike Gen5.1 setup, grids, and reporting surfaces? | [stage-1 packet](../runs/research_workbench/gen4_equivalence/gen4_equivalence_20260706stage1c), [full-symbol packet](../runs/research_workbench/gen4_equivalence/gen4_equivalence_20260707full163q), [wrapper](../scripts/inspect/run_gen4_equivalence_screen.R), [stage-1 deck](../presentations/gen5_gen4_equivalence_stage1.pptx), [full-symbol deck](../presentations/gen5_gen4_equivalence_full16_screen.pptx) | Added a Gen4 artifact forensic wrapper pointed at `FM-002-024-R3_med_16_bins`. It uses the Gen4 `RESEARCH_ASSETS` analogue as behavioral-pool context, `4x4` quantile states, expanding TRAIN from `2016Q4`, and compares Gen5.1 direct-spec against Gen4-style pooled-family selection. Stage 1 used AMD/NVDA/TSLA across `2020Q4`, `2022Q2`, and `2024Q4`, with an 80-row grid reconstructed from supported Gen4-picked strategy labels; direct-spec returned `30.7%` versus equal-weight hold `-15.2%` (`+45.9 pp` alpha), while pooled-family returned `5.0%` (`+20.2 pp`). Follow-up source/artifact audit corrected the Gen5.1 `gen4_daily_default` preset to contain missing active Gen4 artifact values for Bollinger lookback 10, return-z entry 1.5, breakout lookback 10, and nonzero breakout buffers. The full-symbol follow-up added checkpoint-only batched fitting and ran all 16 Gen4 live/reporting symbols over feasible full-symbol quarters `2022Q2` and `2024Q4`; `2020Q4` is only 14-symbol comparable because PLTR/SOFI cannot support the expanding TRAIN range. Full-symbol direct-spec returned `-0.3%` versus local hold `-11.2%` (`+10.9 pp` alpha); pooled-family returned `-8.6%` (`+2.6 pp`). This narrows the setup gap and keeps direct-spec stronger in Gen5.1, but does not reproduce the much larger Gen4 artifact totals. Remaining possible gaps: SMA families and exact Gen4 volatility-breakout semantics. |

Longer labels are not just higher-conviction h1 labels; they ask a different question. Keep `h1 + relative-strength` as the bullish control. If revisiting longer labels, pair them with a minimum-hold replay rule or benchmark-relative/vol-adjusted labels. |

A point-in-time prospectus check corrected the leverage factor from the current 2x objective to NVDL's 2023 1.5x objective before residual construction. Exact NVDL anchor coverage was 1,773/2,250 (78.8%); 177 sessions supplied the full 15:30 comparison. NVDA's prior-close-to-15:30 return had Pearson `-0.042`, Spearman `+0.068`, and a top-minus-bottom-quintile next-30-minute difference of only `+0.069%`, so the proposed same-direction pressure footprint did not appear. The immediately prior 60-minute NVDA return was more positively related to the next 30 minutes at the close (`+0.133`), but that is a separate local-momentum control. NVDL minus 1.5x NVDA tracking residuals showed negative next-window correlations at all clocks, including `-0.419` at 15:30, consistent with ETF relative-price convergence rather than evidence that NVDL moved NVDA. Preserve the primary STOP. No AUM/share reconstruction, inference, costs, rule, performance, or post-2023 NVDA outcome was opened. |

| 224. Alternative Data Lab: GameStop Wikipedia Attention Collection | `COLLECTION_POC_COMPLETE_NO_PREDICTIVE_CLAIM` | Can Gen5 collect, preserve, map, audit, and visualize a simple alternative-data source before opening a predictive or trading question? | [lab](../alternative_data_lab/README.md), [packet](../runs/research_workbench/alternative_data_lab/wikimedia_attention_gamestop_20260901), [report](../runs/research_workbench/alternative_data_lab/wikimedia_attention_gamestop_20260901/report.md), [chart](../runs/research_workbench/alternative_data_lab/wikimedia_attention_gamestop_20260901/visuals/gamestop_wikipedia_pageviews_2019_2023.png), [collector](../alternative_data_lab/R/wikimedia_pageviews.R), [script](../scripts/inspect/run_wikimedia_attention_data_poc.R) | Opened a separate alternative-data lane with a deliberately non-predictive collection POC. The frozen request retrieved all 1,826 daily user-classified page-view observations for the English Wikipedia `GameStop` article from 2019-01-01 through 2023-12-31, preserved the raw JSON and exact request dimensions, emitted a complete calendar and missing-date ledger, and used no market data. No dates were omitted. Median daily attention was 1,048 views; the maximum was 174,157 on 2021-01-28, 166.1x the median. The conspicuous 2021 episode is a positive-control sanity check proving the source can register a real public-attention shock, not evidence that page views led returns. Preserve redirect/title mapping, API latency, and unresolved-zero semantics as part of the source contract. Stop before choosing an attention transformation, threshold, horizon, universe, market join, or trading rule. |
| 225. Alternative Data Lab: GameStop Wikipedia Directional Lead | `STOP_NO_OBVIOUS_ONE_SESSION_DIRECTIONAL_LEAD` | Does a causal surprise in GameStop Wikipedia attention positively lead the next GME session, or does attention mainly document price discovery already underway? | [lab](../alternative_data_lab/README.md), [packet](../runs/research_workbench/alternative_data_lab/wikimedia_attention_lead_gme_20260901), [report](../runs/research_workbench/alternative_data_lab/wikimedia_attention_lead_gme_20260901/report.md), [scatter](../runs/research_workbench/alternative_data_lab/wikimedia_attention_lead_gme_20260901/visuals/attention_reaction_vs_causal_forward.png), [timeline](../runs/research_workbench/alternative_data_lab/wikimedia_attention_lead_gme_20260901/visuals/attention_signal_and_gme_price_timeline.png), [engine](../alternative_data_lab/R/wikimedia_attention_lead.R), [script](../scripts/inspect/run_wikimedia_attention_lead_poc.R) | Froze one continuous feature, `log(views / median(prior 28 complete calendar days))`, and one outcome before inspection. Because the historical endpoint does not preserve actual first-availability timestamps, the UTC measurement day had to end and a fixed 48-hour safeguard had to elapse before the first eligible GME session open; the outcome was exactly the next open-to-open log return. The completed same-session reaction control had Pearson `+0.060`, Spearman `+0.050`, and `n=1,240`, while the causal forward surface had Pearson `-0.102`, Spearman `+0.003`, and `n=1,236`. The essentially zero rank relationship and lack of a positive forward association do not support the obvious directional-lead thesis. This stops only the one-session positive-direction formulation, not all possible uses of attention data. No threshold, horizon search, subperiod selection, trade rule, costs, performance replay, or post-2023 market data were opened. Do not shorten the safeguard after inspection; a faster claim requires prospective receipt-time capture. |
| 226. Alternative Data Lab: GameStop Wikipedia Magnitude Lead + Evidence Deck | `STOP_NO_OBVIOUS_ONE_SESSION_MAGNITUDE_LEAD` | If attention does not predict direction, does the same causally available surprise predict the absolute size of GME's next one-session move? | [deck](../alternative_data_lab/presentations/wikimedia_attention_alternative_data_lab.pptx), [packet](../runs/research_workbench/alternative_data_lab/wikimedia_attention_magnitude_gme_20260901), [report](../runs/research_workbench/alternative_data_lab/wikimedia_attention_magnitude_gme_20260901/report.md), [scatter](../runs/research_workbench/alternative_data_lab/wikimedia_attention_magnitude_gme_20260901/visuals/attention_vs_future_move_magnitude.png), [bin shape](../runs/research_workbench/alternative_data_lab/wikimedia_attention_magnitude_gme_20260901/visuals/attention_deciles_vs_future_magnitude.png), [engine](../alternative_data_lab/R/wikimedia_attention_magnitude.R), [script](../scripts/inspect/run_wikimedia_attention_magnitude_poc.R) | Reused the frozen 28-day attention surprise, UTC-day endpoint plus 48-hour safeguard, first eligible GME open, and one-session boundary. The primary response was absolute next-open log return; entry-session `log(high / low)` was the sole convergent diagnostic. Completed same-session magnitude was visibly associated with attention (Pearson `+0.531`, Spearman `+0.138`), but the causal forward surfaces were outlier-sensitive rather than broadly ordered: absolute return had Pearson `+0.231` and Spearman `+0.006`; high-low range had Pearson `+0.317` and Spearman `-0.008`, each at `n=1,236`. Ten equal-count attention bins were non-monotone and used only to visualize shape. Preserve the stop: the obvious historical one-session feature did not reliably predict either direction or magnitude. No inference, threshold, extra outcome, horizon search, volume variant, trade, costs, performance replay, or post-2023 data were opened. The 11-slide deck records collection, the causal clock, both stops, and the recommendation to require prospective receipt-time capture for any future faster-clock hypothesis. |

## Current Research Posture

The current most useful branch of the POC pathway is:

1. For true 2016-capable temporal replication, use `AMD,NVDA,TSLA,AAPL,MSTR` as the high-beta active/research/tradeable set unless the operator explicitly revises it; use `QQQ,SMH,XLK,XLE,XLF` as the ETF/sector proxy set when testing whether conclusions generalize beyond single-name high beta.
2. Keep active-plus-risk/broad-risk context as a useful baseline, but do not assume it is the only context worth testing. The context-philosophy screen suggests like-for-like and diverse behavioral context can materially change the selection-policy readout.
3. Keep behavioral-pool PCA as the leading PCA panel mode unless a future contextual-snapshot replication slice earns a niche.
4. Treat 3x3 quantile grid as the cleaner default state map for the next replication slice; keep fixed k9 and auto-k max9/max15 as diagnostics and secondary contenders rather than defaults.
5. Use the broader Gen5.1 research candidate-family set with the corrected Gen4 `daily_default` parameter-grid breadth for the next research screens unless the operator explicitly opens a different strategy-grid comparison. The preset now contains all active, implemented non-SMA values exported in the Gen4 daily-default artifact grid. Keep the live advice bridge narrower by family and Gen4-like by design.
6. Continue using portfolio accounting only as a downstream inspection layer. The newest Gen5.2 Gen4 calibration replay is still a Phase40-style artifact/replay calibration surface, not yet a true live-capital portfolio accounting packet.
7. Keep the temporary live bridge frozen to its Gen5.1 authority-consumption contract unless the operator explicitly opens a live-bridge behavior change. The live direct-spec lane consumes `bridge_selected_states.csv`; it is not a place to test newer Gen5.2 direct-selection mechanics.
8. During the temporary bridge period, inspect both live selection-policy lanes daily; the operator-declared practical reading rule is AMD under Gen4-style pooled-family and the remaining live basket under frozen Gen5.1 direct-spec.
9. Treat basket composition and context philosophy as active research axes. The latest context-philosophy screen suggests pooled-family can help high-beta baskets when context is matched or diverse, while direct-spec remains competitive for ETF/sector proxies and high-beta broad-risk context.
10. Add local benchmark-relative diagnostics to the next selection/context screen. The newest benchmark and tape-audit pass showed that positive strategy replay returns can still lag equal-weight buy-and-hold because the model is underexposed during strong upside windows; alpha consistency needs to be inspected separately from raw tactical profitability.
11. Keep the compact `ema_trend` participation variants as a targeted troubleshooting contender, but do not add the broad all-combinations EMA probe to default research grids. The compact EMA-only result suggests more participation can help on average, but window-level deltas are mixed and require a full-family confirmation before promotion.
12. Keep the Gen4-equivalence path staged but updated: the full 16-symbol feasible-window replay has now run, and the fallback probe shows that missing asset/state winners were a real Gen4 mechanic but not sufficient to recover Gen4 behavior. The replay-semantics lab confirms that state-switch continuation is mechanically coherent and materially improves the fallback lane in 2024Q4. The non-SOFI generalization screen then shows continuation has useful pockets, especially high-beta pooled-family lanes, but is uneven enough that `fresh_signal_only` should remain the default while `state_switch_continuation` stays an explicit research factor.
13. The newest style-diversified live-capital screen is the cleanest generalization surface for the current Gen5.2 mechanics question. It shows that direct/pooled and fresh/continuation lanes can help in specific pockets, but no lane beat equal-weight basket hold across both tested windows. The next narrow test should target participation quality during favorable regimes rather than simply increasing exposure everywhere.
14. Treat the live advice bridge as operational continuity infrastructure. Research branches can move fast, but live bridge selection/replay/continuity semantics should remain fixed and provenance-stamped until a live-bridge change is explicitly approved.
15. Treat the Gen5.3 bullish momentum specialist as the current PCA research control surface, but do not keep adding downstream actions just to compensate for weak state discrimination. The first baseline showed useful drawdown avoidance but insufficient rebound participation; the first feature-set challenger showed that feature quality matters. The annual context-size screen showed that longer OOS windows are more informative, while the continuity rerun showed that independent quarterly stitching was too optimistic. The EMA feature diagnostic suggests `reversion_breakout_context` can improve participation under EMA-only rules, but trend/breakout and mean-reversion reopenings did not fix upside capture. State-only exposure improved 2020 rally participation, but both binary and three-action state policies stayed too exposed in 2022. The remaining PCA question is upstream feature/state validity for risk-off separation.
16. Gen5.4 is now an accepted supervised-ML planning fork. `ML-P0` proved the adjusted daily OHLCV feature/label table and leakage audit. `ML-P1` proved the GLM model/replay surface but showed that fixed thresholds were too defensive/selective for rally participation. `ML-P1b` proved that threshold policy matters, but also that more permissive TRAIN-only thresholds do not close the 2020 alpha gap and can weaken 2022 defense. `ML-P1c` showed that `h1` is the best GLM label horizon so far, but probability ranking is still weak. `ML-P2` showed that a conservative seeded XGBoost challenger can improve replay behavior versus GLM while still failing to beat 2020 basket hold or solve global AUC. `ML-P2b` showed that TRAIN-only XGBoost parameter selection does not improve OOS replay or ranking. `ML-P3` showed that relative-strength features remain the best replay control, asset-only is the best defensive control, and broad direct context can improve 2020 ranking slightly while hurting replay. `ML-P4` showed that `h5`/`h10` labels are not simply higher-conviction `h1`; they can improve some 2022 defensive ranking diagnostics but under daily-rescore replay they reduce 2020 upside participation. `ML-P5` showed that universe architecture matters, especially broad-pool transfer for some defensive and SPY/ETF timing pockets, but broader context/research pools alone do not solve high-beta bull participation. `ML-P6` then paused model tuning and established a leakage-safe swing-feature audit: its 34 declared features and h10 alpha-oriented targets are a coherent exploration surface, but individual TRAIN-only relationships are modest and no feature is promoted. The next high-signal slice should use a compact predeclared feature set to compare relative h10 against the current absolute h1 control under the same XGBoost/replay guardrails.
17. The theory-first Gen5.4 market-only fork has now produced two valid STOPs before model fitting. The primitive POC rejected the required opportunity-breadth mechanism, and the fresh leadership × participation confirmation found only `2 / 6` correctly ordered quarters with negative A−B and cost-aware selection-excess. Treat these as falsification evidence. Do not resume the previously contemplated compact XGBoost comparison or mine nearby OHLCV interactions until a separate theory session selects one genuinely distinct, point-in-time information family.
18. The cross-sectional pivot has now completed X0 and X1. The 24-stock panel passed coverage and leakage checks, but only group-relative 20-session momentum cleared all frozen measurement gates. Because the ladder requires two economically distinct primitives, X2 pooled linear modeling remains closed. Treat the result as evidence that panel breadth improves measurement but does not compensate for a narrow information set. The next Gen5.4 gate is selection of one retail-accessible, point-in-time non-price family with an auditable historical timestamp contract.
19. Earnings and filed fundamentals passed the theory/documentation admission gate, and the operator opened the five-company SEC F0. The current environment is externally blocked by Akamai HTTP 403 before any SEC payload is accepted. Preserve SEC as the intended authority, keep Alpaca as the OHLCV authority, and do not substitute a mirror or current-snapshot vendor. The next action is an official-data access remedy, not feature design.
20. The OHLCV-only X1b/C0 extension added one legitimate ranking primitive but did not solve long-entry permission. Group-relative momentum plus intraday-minus-overnight structure now form two distinct ranking candidates, yet none of the four C0 conditions passed. Keep the two-stage demonstrator, model fitting, and policy replay closed. The next theory session should ask whether exposure permission needs a different target, horizon, or information family; it must not reverse the observed C0 signs or tune thresholds on these OOS results.
21. C1 separated risk forecasting from return prediction, but no OHLCV stress primitive ordered realized risk at both frozen horizons. SPY drawdown is a credible h5 risk diagnostic, not yet an exposure policy: it passed h5 and failed h20. Keep continuous scaling closed until a theory session decides whether the scaler is intentionally short-horizon, whether the h20 requirement remains essential, or whether an independent macro/credit risk family is required. Do not make that decision by retuning C1 on its inspected folds.
22. C2 established that official VIX contains multi-horizon risk ordering that is not merely SPY drawdown in disguise, but the simple TRAIN-median conversion into high/low exposure states is unstable. This is evidence for a continuous risk-measurement lane, not authority for a continuous scaler. Keep scaling closed. A future theory session may predeclare a frozen calibration form and test it only on untouched post-2024 data; it must not search thresholds or mappings on the inspected 2020-2024 folds.
23. The Alpaca N0/I0 capability packet proved that historical news is accessible and auditable through the existing credentials, while historical index values are not authorized for this account. Treat 239 articles and five complete pages as plumbing evidence only. Keep sentiment, text representation, outcome joins, and feature/model research closed until a point-in-time news theory contract is explicitly agreed.
24. N1A passed the frozen archive-admission gates, establishing adequate breadth for a measurement discussion rather than predictive value. The archive is highly non-uniform: a pre-META symbol-key gap remains, the five most-covered names supply 60.15% of associations, and a small stale-update tail cannot represent fresh information. Keep N1B implementation closed until symbol-history treatment, a stale-update rule, and symbol-local TRAIN-only normalization are frozen.
25. N1B now has a frozen issuer-relative uncertainty contract, but N1L is only a partial prospective-equivalence pass. Transport, authentication, subscription, deliberate reconnect, receipt-time capture, REST pagination, and reconciliation all passed; no live candidate article was observed. Prefer a later bounded shadow observation during an active US news window over accepting the limitation by assumption. Keep the news/outcome join closed.

26. The later N1L premarket shadow observation captured and REST-reconciled one complete live article while all 13 transport gates passed again. This removes the operational live-path STOP from the frozen N1B measurement POC. Preserve local receipt time as prospective authority because it led provider `created_at` metadata by about six seconds. No predictive, exposure, allocation, or live-advice claim follows from this pass.

27. N1B passed its frozen uncertainty-association gate: all 12 OOS quarters had positive issuer-local rank ordering and positive high-intensity separation, with mean fold Spearman 0.107321. Preserve the pass as evidence that news intensity carries stable information about future path width, not direction. The next gate is theory-first representation design; do not promote the frozen p80 boundary directly into a policy or tune sentiment, thresholds, horizons, models, exposure, allocation, or PnL on these folds.

28. N1C passed its frozen nonredundancy gate. Conditional fold-level ordering
remained positive in all 12 quarters with mean partial Spearman `0.087842`,
after controlling prior h5 path volatility and current dollar-volume surprise.
Preserve the result as modest incremental uncertainty context, not a causal,
directional, or trading claim. The next gate is a theory-first decision about
one predeclared recency-weighted representation challenger; implementation
remains closed until its economic role and leakage-safe form are agreed.

29. N1D stopped the recency-representation path on fresh confirmation data.
All integrity checks passed and recency remained positively associated with
future h5 uncertainty, but it did not improve on the simpler equal-count
baseline. Preserve the lesson that numerical granularity and tie resolution
are not predictive value. Retain equal count as descriptive uncertainty
context and close news-representation expansion; do not tune alternate decay,
sentiment, source weighting, embeddings, models, policy, exposure, allocation,
PnL, or live behavior on these inspected outcomes.

30. E0 passed point-in-time event construction without reopening news
representation. Preserve the operational term `issuer information cycle`,
because same-day articles need not be one semantic event. The accepted raw
archive exactly reproduces 49,590 article-issuer keys and supports every
issuer-quarter, but the source is Benzinga-only. Open only an E1 theory
discussion; do not join price response, assign sentiment, train a model, or
define a trading policy until the reaction and confirmation measurements are
frozen.

31. E1 froze the full observation-to-entry timeline and proved that the
unusual-news reaction signal itself has broad development support, but the
same-issuer zero-news counterfactual is too sparse. Preserve
`STOP_E1_DEVELOPMENT_MECHANICS`: `122 / 465`, or `26.2%`, matched against the
frozen `70%` requirement. Do not rescue the test by widening its `0.50z`
caliper or changing zero-news after seeing the result. The next step is an
operator-owned theory choice between a same-issuer ordinary-news control and a
predeclared economic-peer zero-news control; neither is yet authorized.

32. T1 implemented the first mechanism-first detour exactly as frozen. All
coverage, timing, explicit-as-of, adjusted-bar, cost, and next-open integrity
checks passed, but the persistence mechanism did not: confirmation ON-minus-OFF
was `-29.71 bp`, only `5 / 14` assets had positive full-history separation,
and T1 trailed its exposure-matched control. Preserve the `72.5%` drawdown
reduction as risk-shaping evidence, not selection alpha. The next recommended
conversation is a fresh M1 cross-sectional momentum contract; do not implement
M1 or retune T1 without an explicit operator gate.

33. M1 was implemented exactly as frozen and stopped at its ranking gate.
Integrity, timing, support, and common-session coverage passed, but only
`2 / 6` M1A gates passed: repeated monthly and quarterly ordering missed their
minimums, observed top-K excess missed the randomized p90, and positive
contribution was dominated by emerging-country ETFs. Preserve
`STOP_M1_RANKING_MECHANISM`. M1B was structurally not run; do not compute or
infer M1 portfolio performance or rescue the mechanism with a nearby horizon,
subset, group-neutralization rule, or tuned concentration cap.

34. S0 is conceptually approved as the third mechanism-first detour and now
has an exact proposed contract. It tests residual convergence across a fixed
37-ETF, four-group survivor panel before any portfolio replay. Preserve the
historical-borrow boundary: Alpaca current borrow metadata cannot be projected
backward as point-in-time shortability. Do not retrieve the panel, inspect
outcomes, fit pairs, run random controls, compute portfolio metrics, or place
short orders until the exact universe, timing, thresholds, ordinary costs,
controls, and nine gates receive operator approval.

35. The return-geometry lane now has a durable candidate ledger and a separate
daily continuation microscope. `PROTO-MR-01` records the temporally unstable
volume-veto rebound as a proto-mean-reversion strategy; `BEHAV-MOM-01` records
sideways gain continuation as a descriptive behavioral lead. No new outcome
was calculated. Existing evidence shows a weak TSLA 5-10-session ridge, a
clearer TSLA ATR%-conditioned split, 39/81 continuation-support cells in the
30-asset path-sideways state versus 0/81 path-trending, and a small but broad
129-instrument sideways-versus-trending gain-branch contrast. Preserve
`DESCRIPTIVE_BEHAVIORAL_LEAD_STOP_BEFORE_RULE_SELECTION`. The next gate is an
operator-owned narrative choice before any horizon selection, next-open rule,
post-2023 query, or 30-minute calculation. The intraday lane remains
design-only until its bar clock, overnight convention, horizon meaning, and
filter timing are frozen.

36. The operator selected the direct ER20-sideways versus ER20-trending
continuation contrast for fuller context. The slice re-expressed the frozen
2018-2023 full-vocabulary atlas without querying bars or recomputing outcomes.
Within each asset and horizon cell, the positive-prior sideways correlation was
paired against its trending counterpart before equal-sector aggregation. The
all-surface median paired contrast is `+0.048`; `209 / 225` cells favor
sideways, and all 11 sector surfaces have positive median contrasts. The
result is not universal: 16 cells are exceptions, and the lone incomplete
asset pair is RIVN `100 -> 100` trending with only 24 observations, outside the
88-stock headline core. Preserve
`DESCRIPTIVE_PAIRED_STATE_CONTRAST_COMPLETE_STOP_BEFORE_HORIZON_OR_RULE_SELECTION`.
No horizon, next-open rule, baseline, post-2023 outcome, or 30-minute
calculation was opened.

37. The continuation lead has now been translated into a literal causal daily
next-open proto-rule. Positive R20 in ER20-sideways produced positive raw net
returns at 5, 10, and 20 sessions, but trailed unconditional same-horizon drift
at every hold. Only the 20-session sideways-versus-trending contrast remained
positive under both equal-sector and event-pooled lenses, while the positive-
R20 condition detracted versus sideways-only. Preserve
`TRAIN_NEXT_OPEN_HORIZON_COMPARISON_COMPLETE_STOP_BEFORE_SELECTION_OR_OOS`.
Do not select 20 sessions, tune thresholds, open portfolio replay, or query
post-2023 outcomes. The next gate is an operator huddle on whether the state
contrast merits a separately frozen mechanism test without the sign condition.
The 30-minute lane remains sealed for later.

## Next Candidate Tests

- Run the older-window temporal context batch as an overnight job over `AMD,NVDA,TSLA,AAPL,MSTR`, starting with adjacent 2021 and 2022 windows; resolve the `VXX` pre-2018 context choice before claiming older fully comparable windows.
- Use the context-philosophy screen as the next inspection surface: compare high-beta versus ETF/sector behavior, and decide whether the next narrow slice should add risk-adjusted portfolio accounting, test a third basket archetype, or revisit PCA state-grid size under the most informative context recipes.
- Revisit contextual snapshot as a narrow challenger only after the behavioral-pool 3x3 context result is stable enough to justify another panel-mode comparison.
- Inspect k-means warning/convergence behavior before treating any fixed-k or auto-k outperformance as actionable research evidence.
- If selection-policy uncertainty remains important, run a deliberately scoped next screen that treats `asset_state_direct_spec` versus `pooled_family_asset_variant` as an explicit factor alongside one or two already-promising context/state settings; do not silently swap policy defaults.
- Decide whether to retire ex-active market-risk context from default grids while keeping it as an occasional diagnostic/control.
- Use the Gen5.3 annual continuity, EMA feature diagnostic, trend/breakout reopening, mean-reversion reopening, state-only, and three-action packets as the current high-beta specialist control surface. The next narrow test should target PCA feature/state validity for risk-off separation and bullish hold discrimination; do not assume that simply widening the strategy pool or adding more downstream actions will improve upside capture. Only retest context size/composition if a participation-specific mechanism helps under the fixed control context.
- Hold the post-ML-P6 compact swing-model comparison, cross-sectional X2, the two-stage rules demonstrator, and continuous risk scaling. Resolve official SEC access when practical. C2 removes the immediate need to force an h5-only controller: VIX showed continuous ordering at both horizons, but its median state failed. The next theory session should decide whether to predeclare one monotone calibration and reserve post-2024 data for untouched confirmation, or stop the risk-policy lane; do not tune a threshold or nonlinear map on C2's inspected folds.
- If the non-price lane is revisited after N1D, begin only from a genuinely new
  economic hypothesis and fresh confirmation data. Preserve the accepted
